| POST | `/bookings` | `{ name, email, company?, message?, locale, startsAt, website? }` (`website`: honeypot) | `201 { id, startsAt, meetingUrl }`; `409` when the time isn't free (any more). Emails the visitor (their language, Meet link, cancel link, invitation) and the team inbox (invitation, reply-to the visitor) — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/bookings/cancel` | `{ token }` (from the visitor's email) | `200 { startsAt }`; `404` for an unknown or already cancelled call. Emails the team |
| POST | `/forms/:key` | A client site's contact form: `{ name, email, phone?, message, website? (honeypot), redirect? }`, urlencoded (a plain HTML form) or JSON | Emails the project's `recipients` (Spanish, reply-to the visitor); with a managed site it also keeps the message (`SiteFormMessage`) and the email links to it in the portal. A form post is answered `303` to `redirect` or the `Referer` with `form=ok` / `form=error` added, only when that URL is on the project's production or staging origin (else a short page); JSON gets `{ ok }`. `403` when `Origin` is another site; `404` for an unknown or disabled key — limited to 5/min/IP |
| GET | `/domains/availability?name=` | A label (`peluqueria-ana`, accents and spaces become dashes) or a domain | `{ results: [{ domain, available: true \| false \| null }] }`: a label in `.com`, `.es` and `.eu`; a domain first, then its label in the other two. OVH's order API answers when its keys are set; otherwise RDAP (404 free, 200 taken; `null` when unknown, e.g. `.es`). Cached 10 min, 400 without a usable label, limited to 20/min/IP |
| POST | `/site-requests` | The basic-website request: `{ preset, business: { name, kind, city, address?, phone?, whatsapp?, email?, hours? (a line per slot, "Days: hours"), services? (a line each, "Service — price"), about?, wishes? (what they want on the website, in their words) }, domain?: { name, owned }, name, email, locale, website? (honeypot) }` | `201 { id, previewUrl? }`: a lead (`projectType: web`, `company` = the business, the message a readable summary with the link to the preview's waiting page) keeping `siteRequest` (with the domain they want, `owned: false`, or have); onboarding pre-fills the site's domain with it; the usual lead emails. No photos (decision 13). Limited to 3/min/IP. Onboarding with that lead fills the site from it: business name and tagline, contact, address, hours, services and about, `schemaType: LocalBusiness`; the preset's made-up services, reviews and facts are dropped |
| GET | `/meetings/rooms/by-code/:code` | `{ code, title, ownerName, live }` of a meeting room (section 4.8); `404` when there is none, `400` for something that can't be a code. Limited to 60/min/IP |
| POST | `/meetings/rooms/:code/guest-ticket` | `{ name }` (2–40) → `{ ticket, expiresIn: 30, iceServers, room: { code, title } }`: a guest's single-use ticket into the room. Limited to 10/min/IP |
| GET | `/citas/p/:slug` | A booking page (section 4.9): `{ slug, title, description, ownerName, durationMinutes, location, video, askPhone, timeZone, enabled, slots }` (`slots`: free starts, ISO); `404` when there is none |
| POST | `/citas/p/:slug/appointments` | `{ startsAt, name, email, phone?, note?, locale, website? }` → the appointment `{ id, slug, title, ownerName, startsAt, endsAt, timeZone, location, meetingCode, status }`; `400` without a required phone, `409` when paused or the time isn't free. Limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/citas/cancel/preview` | `{ token }` → that appointment; `404` for an unknown token |
| POST | `/citas/cancel` | `{ token }` → the cancelled appointment (again if already cancelled); `409` once it has started |
| POST | `/swipe/takedown` | `{ email, photoRef?, reason, details, locale, website? }` → `{ id }`: a removal request (section 4.10); a matching `photoRef` hides that photo at once. Limited to 5/min/IP |
| GET | `/cruising/spots` | `?bbox=minLng,minLat,maxLng,maxLat&type=` (`bbox` required, `minLng` < `maxLng`, `minLat` < `maxLat`, longitudes −180..180, latitudes −90..90, else `400`; `type` optional, one of the types; phase 5: `tags=NUDIST,LIT`, only spots with all of them) → `{ items: CruisingSpotSummary[], truncated }`: up to 500 LIVE spots, best rated first. `Cache-Control: public, max-age=60`. 120/min/IP (section 4.15) |
| GET | `/cruising/spots/:slug` | `CruisingSpotPublic`; `404` unless LIVE |
| GET | `/cruising/places` | `{ countries: [{ countryCode, count, cities: [{ city, citySlug, count }] }] }`: LIVE spots only, most spots first |
| GET | `/cruising/places/:countryCode/:citySlug` | `{ countryCode, city, citySlug, items: CruisingSpotSummary[] }` (up to 200, best rated first); `404` when the city has no LIVE spot |
| GET | `/cruising/sitemap` | `{ items: [{ slug, countryCode, citySlug, updatedAt }] }` of LIVE spots, for the website's sitemap |
| POST | `/cruising/takedown` | `{ email, spotLink?, mediaRef?, reason, details, locale, website? }` (`reason` MINOR · ILLEGAL · NON_CONSENSUAL · STOLEN · DANGER · OTHER) → `{ id }`: a removal request (section 4.15); a `spotLink` (a URL ending in `/spots/<slug>`, or the slug) of a LIVE spot hides it at once when `reason` is MINOR or ILLEGAL; a `mediaRef` (phase 4, the 8-character ref of a photo or video, any case) hides the item at once for MINOR, ILLEGAL, NON_CONSENSUAL or STOLEN. Limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/csp-reports` | One `report-uri` report `{ "csp-report": { … } }` or a Reporting API list (≤ 20) → 204: the website forwards what browsers report about its Content Security Policy (sections 4.11, 6.4). Limited to 20/min/IP; stores no IP |
| POST | `/errors` | `{ source: server \| browser, message, stack?, method?, path?, digest?, userAgent? }` → 204: the website reports an error (section 4.11). Limited to 30/min/IP |
| GET | `/videochat/online` | Public: `{ online }`, people connected to the video chat right now (cached 10 s). Limited to 60/min/IP |
| GET | `/site-requests/previews/:slug` | Public: whether a request's preview is online → `{ status, url, business, purchase }`; `purchase` is `{ priceCents (before VAT), vatPercent, totalCents, currency, available }` while buying online is on (`available`: READY, not bought, not a client yet), else null. 404 when there is none. Limited to 30/min/IP |
| GET | `/site-maintenance/offer` | Public: the maintenance plan for the landing (section 8.2) → `{ available, price: { amountCents, currency, interval, vatPercent } \| null, allowance: 3 }`; `available` false (and `price` null) while it is off |
| POST | `/site-requests/previews/:slug/checkout` | Public: `{ locale, accept: true }` (the terms and starting at once) → `{ url }`, a Stripe Checkout Session to pay the website (section 8.2). 404 while buying online is off or without a preview; 409 when it isn't READY, was bought or its lead is a client. Limited to 5/min/IP |
| GET | `/site-requests/purchases/:sessionId` | Public: how a purchase is going, for the page Stripe returns to → `{ status, business, siteUrl? }` (`siteUrl` once its site exists); 404 when unknown |
| POST | `/payments/stripe` | Stripe's webhook (raw body, `Stripe-Signature` checked with `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, else 400): a paid `checkout.session.completed` or `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` marks the purchase PAID and makes the site; `checkout.session.expired` marks it EXPIRED. Answers 200 to anything else |
| POST | `/site-audits` | The free website check (section 4.12): `{ url, email, name?, locale, acceptPrivacy: true, website? (honeypot) }`; `url` without a scheme gets `https://` | `202 { id }`, the check runs in the background (the same email and host within 24 hours gives the earlier `id`); `400` field error `url` for an address that can't be checked; `429` past 5 a day per email — limited to 3/min/IP |
| GET | `/site-audits/:id` | `?locale=en` → `SiteAuditReport` `{ id, url, finalUrl, status, score, failure (why it couldn't be checked, FAILED only), categories: [{ key: speed \| mobile \| seo \| security, name, score, checks: [{ id, status: pass \| warn \| fail, title, advice }] }] (worst first), createdAt, finishedAt }` (texts in `locale`; no email or name); `404` when unknown. Limited to 60/min/IP |
| POST | `/newsletter` | `{ email, locale, acceptPrivacy: true, website? (honeypot) }` | `202` always, whether or not the address is subscribed (section 4.14). A new, `PENDING` or `UNSUBSCRIBED` address is emailed a link to `SITE_URL/{locale}/newsletter/confirm?token=…` (48 hours; at most one email per address every 10 minutes); an `ACTIVE` one is sent nothing; a filled honeypot does nothing. `400` field error `email: disposable` for a disposable address — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/newsletter/confirm` | `{ token }` | `200 { email, locale }`: the subscriber is `ACTIVE`. `400` when the token is unknown or expired — limited to 10/min/IP |
| POST | `/newsletter/unsubscribe` | `{ token }` (the subscriber's `unsubscribeToken`, from any newsletter email) | `200 { email }`, also when already unsubscribed; `404` for an unknown token — limited to 10/min/IP |
| POST | `/newsletter/unsubscribe/:token` | One-click unsubscribe (RFC 8058): body `List-Unsubscribe=One-Click`, urlencoded; it is the address in the emails' `List-Unsubscribe` header | `200`, same effect as above; `404` for an unknown token — limited to 60/min/IP |
| GET | `/newsletter/unsubscribe/:token` | For mail clients that open the `List-Unsubscribe` address in a browser | `302` to `SITE_URL/{locale}/newsletter/unsubscribe?token=…` (the subscriber's language; `en` for an unknown token); changes nothing — limited to 30/min/IP |
| GET | `/calculator` | The calculator's prices (`Setting` `calculator`) once published; `404` until then | |
| POST | `/drafts` | `Authorization: Bearer <release key>` + `{ title, body, version?, environment? (required with a version), url? }` | `201 UpdateDraft` (`{ id, project: { id, name }, title, body, version, environment, url, createdAt }`); the team inbox is emailed a link to review it. Same key rules and limit as `/releases` |
| POST | `/releases` | `Authorization: Bearer <release key>` + the body of `POST /admin/projects/:id/releases` | `201` release on the key's project, posted as "Despliegue automático" (shown as Codeqlick); `401` for a missing, unknown or revoked key — limited to 20/min/IP. For deploy pipelines: see below |
| GET | `/media/:filename` | | The uploaded image, cacheable forever (names are never reused); 404 otherwise |
| POST | `/auth/login` | `{ email, password, captcha? }` (`captcha`: the Turnstile token, required while `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` is set; `400` field error `captcha` when refused) | `200 AuthTokens`, or `200 { twoFactorRequired: true, challengeToken }` when two-step verification is on — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/2fa/verify` | `{ challengeToken, code }` (authenticator or recovery code) | `200 AuthTokens`; `401` for a wrong code or an expired challenge — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/passkeys/login/options` | — → `{ options, challengeId }` (discoverable credentials; section 4.3, decision 104) — limited to 20/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/passkeys/login/verify` | `{ challengeId, response }` → `200 AuthTokens` (no two-step verification step); `401` when it doesn't verify, the passkey is unknown or the account is inactive — limited to 10/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/refresh` | `{ refreshToken }` | `200 AuthTokens` |
| POST | `/auth/password-reset/request` | `{ email, locale }` | `202` always, whether or not the account exists. An active account is emailed a link to `SITE_URL/{locale}/reset-password?token=…`, or, when it was never registered, the invitation to create it — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/register` | `{ name, email, password, locale, acceptPrivacy: true, website? (honeypot), captcha? }` (`captcha` as in login) | `202` always (it never says whether the email has an account); `400` field error `email: disposable` for a disposable address, `password: breached` for a password found in a data breach (4.3), `captcha` for a refused token. An email without a registered account is emailed a link to `SITE_URL/{locale}/register/confirm?token=…` (24 hours); one with a registered account is emailed that it already has one, with links to sign in and reset the password — limited to 3/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/register/confirm` | `{ token }` | `200 AuthTokens`: the account is created (role CLIENT) or, when the team had prepared one for that email, it becomes theirs with the name and password they chose; signed in at once. `400` when the token is unknown, used or expired — limited to 5/min/IP |
| GET | `/auth/register/invitation?token=…` | | `{ email, name }` of the invitation (to fill in the form); `400` when invalid or expired |
| POST | `/auth/register/invitation` | `{ token, name, password, locale, acceptPrivacy: true }` | `200 AuthTokens`: the invitation's link proves the email, so the account is theirs at once; `400` field error `password: breached` (4.3) — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/password-reset/confirm` | `{ token, newPassword }` | `204`: password set, token spent, every session of the user revoked. `400` when the token is unknown, used or expired, or field error `newPassword: breached` (4.3; the token stays valid) — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/email/unsubscribe?token=` | The one-click unsubscribe mail providers call from `List-Unsubscribe` (RFC 8058, section 4.3); the body (form-urlencoded `List-Unsubscribe=One-Click`) is ignored | `204`: the token's preference (`digest` → `weeklyDigest`, `report` → `monthlyReport`) is off; the same when it already was. `404` for an invalid token or a deleted account (one answer for both) — limited to 120/min/IP (mail providers call from shared addresses) |
| GET | `/email/unsubscribe?token=` | The same URL opened in a browser | `302` to `SITE_URL/{locale}/email/unsubscribe?token=…` (the account's language, `en` when the token is invalid, the page then says so); changes nothing — limited to 30/min/IP |
| GET | `/email/preferences?token=` | | `{ kind: digest \| report, subscribed, email }` with the email masked (`l***@example.com`); changes nothing (link scanners open it). `404` as above — limited to 30/min/IP |
| PUT | `/email/preferences` | `{ token, subscribed: boolean }` | `{ kind, subscribed, email }` (masked): the website's "Unsubscribe" and "Undo" buttons. `404` as above — limited to 30/min/IP |

### Authenticated (any role)

| Method | Path | Response |
|---|---|---|
| POST | `/auth/logout` | `{ refreshToken }` → `204` |
| GET | `/auth/me` | `AuthUser` |
| GET / PATCH | `/auth/preferences` | `{ weeklyDigest, monthlyReport }` / either of them â the preferences |
| GET | `/auth/2fa` | `{ enabled, recoveryCodesLeft }` |
| POST | `/auth/2fa/setup` | `{ secret, otpauthUrl }`: a new secret, pending until confirmed; `409` when already on |
| POST | `/auth/2fa/enable` | `{ code }` → `{ recoveryCodes }` (shown once); `400` for a wrong code — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/2fa/disable` | `{ password, code }` → `204`; `400` with a field error for a wrong password or code — limited to 5/min/IP |
| GET | `/auth/passkeys` | `{ items: [{ id, name, deviceType, backedUp, createdAt, lastUsedAt }] }` (section 4.3, decision 104) |
| POST | `/auth/passkeys/register/options` | `{ password }` → WebAuthn creation options (`400` field error for a wrong password, `409` past 10) — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/passkeys/register/verify` | `{ response, name? }` (the browser's attestation) → `201` the passkey; `400` when it doesn't verify or the challenge expired |
| PATCH | `/auth/passkeys/:id` | `{ name }` → the passkey; `404` when not mine |
| DELETE | `/auth/passkeys/:id` | `{ password }` → `204`; `400` wrong password, `404` when not mine — limited to 5/min/IP |
| GET | `/auth/sessions` | `ActiveSession[]`: `{ id, userAgent, ip, lastActiveAt, current }`, one per signed-in device, most recent first (`id` is the family id) |
| DELETE | `/auth/sessions/:id` | `204`: signs that device out; `404` when it isn't the caller's or has already ended |
| POST | `/auth/sessions/revoke-others` | `{ refreshToken }` → `204`: signs out every device except the one owning `refreshToken` |
| POST | `/auth/password` | `{ currentPassword, newPassword, refreshToken }` → `204`. Revokes the caller's other sessions; the one owning `refreshToken` stays signed in. `400` with a field error when `currentPassword` is wrong, `newPassword` (min 12 chars) equals it, or `newPassword: breached` (4.3) — limited to 5/min/IP |
| PATCH | `/auth/me` | `{ name?, locale? }` → `AuthUser`: the caller's own name and email language |
| GET | `/auth/username/available` | `?username=` → `{ username, available, reason? }` (`reason`: `invalid` · `reserved` · `taken`; the caller's own name counts as available). 30/min |
| POST · DELETE | `/auth/me/avatar` | POST multipart `file` (PNG, JPEG or WebP up to 10 MB) → `AuthUser` with `avatar`: re-encoded to a 256×256 WebP square without metadata, replacing the old one; `400` not an image. DELETE → `AuthUser` without it. 10/min |
| GET | `/avatars/:file` | Public: a profile photo (`image/webp`, cached for a year: names are random and never reused) |
| PUT | `/auth/me/username` | `{ username }` (3–20 characters: `a-z`, `0-9`, `.` and `_`, starting and ending with a letter or digit, no `..`; upper case is lowered) → `AuthUser` with `username`; `400` invalid or reserved, `409` taken by someone else. `null` removes it. 10/min |
| POST | `/auth/email` | `{ email, password, locale }` → `202` (`400` field error `email: disposable` for a disposable address): emails a link to the new address (`SITE_URL/{locale}/confirm-email?token=…`, 24 hours; a new request replaces the pending one). `400` with a field error for a wrong password or the same email; `409` when the email already has an account — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/auth/email/confirm` | `{ token }` (no sign-in needed: the link may open on another device) → `{ email }`: the account's email changes, every session ends (they sign in again with the new address) and the old address is told. `400` when the token is unknown, used or expired; `409` when the email got an account meanwhile — limited to 5/min/IP |
| GET | `/auth/me/export` | Everything the platform keeps about the caller, as a JSON download (`codeqlick-<date>.json`; GDPR access and portability): account, sessions, projects they take part in, proposals, support requests and messages, comments on updates, managed-site edits, leads that became their account, meeting rooms, booking pages with their appointments, video chat profile, likes, blocks, reports made and sanctions. Never other people's data beyond names of the team and project titles. Limited to 3/hour |
| DELETE | `/auth/me` | `{ password }` → `204`: the account and everything that hangs from it is deleted (as an admin deleting it) and a goodbye email is sent. Refused: `403` for admin accounts (another admin deletes them); `409` for a client account that owns projects (contracts and invoices must be kept: the page offers a support request to close it). A person of a client company deletes just themself. `400` with a field error for a wrong password — limited to 5/min/IP |

| GET | `/videochat/me` | `{ onboarded, termsVersion, acceptedTermsVersion, suspended, suspension, ageVerificationRequired, ageVerified, allowed, profile, pushPublicKey, peakAlerts }` (section 4.7; `pushPublicKey` is the VAPID key, null while notifications are off; `peakAlerts` whether the account asked for peak time alerts); `suspension` is `{ reason, until, appeal: { status, response, createdAt } \| null }` or null, `profile` is `VideochatSettings` or null before onboarding |
| PUT | `/videochat/profile` | `VideochatSettings` `{ nickname, gender, seeking[], country, language, sameCountryOnly, sameLanguageOnly, interests[] }` (interests: up to 5 of the fixed list, section 4.7) → the same; `409` before onboarding |
| PUT | `/videochat/push` | `{ endpoint, keys: { p256dh, auth }, locale }` (a browser's push subscription) → `204`; `409` before onboarding or while notifications are off — limited to 10/min/IP |
| DELETE | `/videochat/push` | `{ endpoint }` → `204` (also when unknown) |
| PUT | `/videochat/push/peak` | `{ on }` → `{ on }`: peak time alerts for the account (section 4.7); `409` before onboarding |
| GET | `/videochat/stats` | `{ week: { chats, likes, contacts }, previousWeek }`: the person's last 7 days and the 7 before (section 4.7) |
| POST | `/videochat/verification` | multipart `file` → the status (`verification: { status: PENDING, note }`); `409` without a nickname, when verified already, before onboarding or while suspended; `400` not an image; `429` over 5 a day |
| GET | `/videochat/verification/file` | My photo while it waits for review |
| GET | `/videochat/contacts` | `{ items: [{ contactId, nickname, gender, country, language, interests, verified, online, busy, since }] }`: mutual contacts, newest first (section 4.7) |
| DELETE | `/videochat/contacts/:id` | → `204`: removes my like, so we are no longer contacts |
| POST | `/videochat/appeal` | `{ message }` → `204`; `409` when not suspended or the suspension was already appealed — limited to 5/min/IP |
| PUT | `/videochat/me` | `{ birthDate: 'YYYY-MM-DD', acceptTerms: true, termsVersion }` → the status; `403` under 18 (nothing stored), `409` for an old `termsVersion` — limited to 10/min/IP |
| GET | `/meetings/rooms` | `{ items: [{ id, code, title, lobby, createdAt, lastUsedAt, live }] }`: my meeting rooms, newest first (`live`: people inside now) |
| POST | `/meetings/rooms` | `{ title }` → the room; `409` past 20 rooms |
| PATCH | `/meetings/rooms/:id` | `{ title?, lobby? }` → the room (a live meeting follows); `404` when not mine |
| DELETE | `/meetings/rooms/:id` | `204`; a meeting going on in it ends |
| POST | `/meetings/rooms/:code/ticket` | Like the guest's ticket, with my account's name; the owner's ticket goes straight in |
| GET | `/citas/pages` | `{ items }`: my booking pages, with `upcoming` (confirmed appointments to come) and `meetingCode` |
| POST | `/citas/pages` | A page's fields → the page; `409` for a taken link or past 10 pages |
| PATCH | `/citas/pages/:id` | Any of the fields → the page; `404` when not mine |
| DELETE | `/citas/pages/:id` | `204`; its upcoming appointments are cancelled and their people emailed |
| GET | `/citas/appointments?scope=upcoming\|past` | `{ items }`: appointments on my pages, with the page's title |
| POST | `/citas/appointments/:id/cancel` | The owner cancels one; the visitor is emailed |
| GET | `/swipe/me` | Swipe status (section 4.10): `{ onboarded, termsVersion, acceptedTermsVersion, suspended, suspension: { reason, until } \| null, allowed, profile: { nickname, gender, seeking, interests } \| null, verification: { status, note, hasPhoto }, canUpload }` |
| PUT | `/swipe/me` | `{ birthDate, acceptTerms: true, termsVersion }` → the status; `403` under 18 (nothing stored), `409` for an old `termsVersion`. 10/min |
| PUT | `/swipe/profile` | `{ nickname, gender?, seeking, interests }` → the status |
| POST | `/swipe/verification` | Multipart `file` + `consent=true` → the status (verification PENDING); `400` not an image, `409` without a nickname. 10/min |
| GET | `/swipe/verification/file` | My verification photo (`image/webp`); `404` when none |
| GET | `/swipe/photos` | `{ items: [{ id, ref, status, rejectReason, likes, width, height, inAlbum, createdAt }] }`: my photos |
| POST | `/swipe/photos` | Multipart `file` + `consent=true` (+ `album=true` for the private album) → the photo (PENDING); `400` not an image or a removed one, `403` before the verification is approved, `409` past 10 public photos or 5 album photos, `429` past 10 uploads a day |
| PATCH | `/swipe/photos/:id` | `{ album }` → the photo, moved into or out of the private album (no new review); `409` when the destination is full |
| DELETE | `/swipe/photos/:id` | `204`; the file is deleted |
| GET | `/swipe/photos/:id/file` | The photo (`image/webp`, `Cache-Control: private, max-age=300`) for its owner, admins or a person who may see it in the deck (an album photo: only someone matched with its owner); otherwise `404` |
| GET | `/swipe/deck` | `{ items: [{ id, ref, width, height, nickname, gender, interests, bio, verified, lovesYou }] }`: up to 10 photos to vote on; `403` before onboarding or while suspended |
| POST | `/swipe/votes` | `{ photoId, like }` → `{ match: { id, nickname } \| null }` (a new match only once). 300/h |
| GET | `/swipe/matches` | `{ items: [{ id, partner: { nickname, gender, interests, bio, verified, photoId, albumPhotos }, lastMessage: { text, mine, at } \| null, unread, createdAt }] }` |
| GET | `/swipe/stats` | `{ week: { views, likes, loves, matches }, previousWeek }`: what happened to my photos in the last 7 days and the 7 before |
| GET | `/swipe/matches/:id/album` | `{ items: [{ id, ref, width, height }] }`: the partner's live album photos (empty when either blocked or they are suspended); `404` when the match isn't mine |
| GET | `/swipe/matches/:id/messages` | `?after=<ISO>` → `{ items: [{ id, mine, text, createdAt }] }` (the last 100, or newer than `after`); marks them read; `404` when not mine |
| POST | `/swipe/matches/:id/messages` | `{ text }` (1–1000) → the message. 20/min |
| DELETE | `/swipe/matches/:id` | `204`: ends the match and deletes its chat |
| POST | `/swipe/matches/:id/block` | `204`: ends the match and blocks the person both ways |
| POST | `/swipe/reports` | `{ photoId \| matchId, reason, details? }` → `204`; MINOR, NON_CONSENSUAL and STOLEN hide the photo at once. 20 a day |
| GET | `/cruising/me` | `CruisingStatus` (section 4.15) |
| PUT | `/cruising/me` | `{ birthDate, acceptTerms: true, consentSpecialData: true, termsVersion }` → `CruisingStatus`; `403` under 18 (nothing stored), `409` for an old `termsVersion`. 10/min |
| PATCH | `/cruising/profile` | Any of `{ emailAlerts }`, phase 2 `{ nickname, bio, country, lookingFor, showAge }` phase 3 `{ favoriteAlerts, favoriteEmails }` and phase 6 `{ onboardingDone }` (true: the tour finished or skipped, `onboardingDoneAt` keeps the first time; false: reopened) → `CruisingStatus`; `409` when the nickname is taken or another member left it less than 14 days ago. Needs entry |
| GET | `/cruising/me/spots` | `{ items: [{ id, slug, name, type, status, rejectReason, countryCode, city, ratingAvg, ratingCount, createdAt }] }`: the spots I proposed, newest first |
| POST | `/cruising/spots` | `{ name, type, lat, lng, countryCode, city, description, bestTimes? }` → `{ id, slug, status: 'PENDING' }`; `403` without entry, `429` past 5 a day |
| PATCH | `/cruising/spots/:id` | Any of the fields of `POST` → `{ id, slug, status }`; only my PENDING or REJECTED spot (a REJECTED one goes back to PENDING), else `409`; `404` when not mine |
| DELETE | `/cruising/spots/:id` | `204`; only my PENDING or REJECTED spot (else `409`) |
| GET | `/cruising/spots/:id/reviews` | `?page&pageSize` → `Paginated<CruisingReview>`, newest first; needs entry; `404` unless the spot is LIVE |
| PUT | `/cruising/spots/:id/review` | `{ rating, text? }` → `CruisingReview`: creates my review or replaces it; `400` when the text shares contact details; `404` unless LIVE; 10 a day |
| DELETE | `/cruising/spots/:id/review` | `204`: deletes my review |
| POST | `/cruising/reports` | `{ spotId \| reviewId \| nickname \| conversationId \| mediaId \| meetupId \| alertId, reason, details? }` (exactly one target; `nickname` and `conversationId` are phase 2, `mediaId` phase 4, `meetupId` and `alertId` phase 5) → `204`; `400` for WRONG_INFO without `details` or on anything but a spot, for NON_CONSENSUAL or STOLEN on anything but media or a profile, and for DANGER on media; MINOR and ILLEGAL hide a spot or review at once, MINOR, NON_CONSENSUAL, STOLEN and ILLEGAL hide media at once. 20 a day |
| GET | `/cruising/members/:nickname` | Phase 2. `{ nickname, bio, country, lookingFor, age, memberSince, verified, coverId }` (`age` null unless `showAge`; `verified`, `coverId` phase 4); `404` when unknown, blocked either way or suspended; needs entry and my own nickname |
| GET | `/cruising/check-in` | Phase 2. My active check-in `{ spot: { id, slug, name }, anonymous, expiresAt }`, or `null` |
| POST | `/cruising/check-ins` | Phase 2. `{ spotId, hours: 1 \| 2 \| 3, anonymous }` → the same shape: ends my previous one; `409` without a nickname; `404` unless the spot is LIVE; 10 a day |
| DELETE | `/cruising/check-in` | Phase 2. `204`: ends my check-in |
| GET | `/cruising/spots/:id/presence` | Phase 2. `{ count, members: [{ nickname, since, verified, coverId }] }`: `count` includes anonymous check-ins and mine; `members` only the non-anonymous ones, not blocked; needs entry |
| GET | `/cruising/conversations` | Phase 2. `{ items: [{ id, partner: { nickname, verified, coverId }, lastMessage: { text, mine, at } \| null, unread, createdAt }] }`, latest first |
| POST | `/cruising/conversations` | Phase 2. `{ nickname }` → `{ id }`: opens the conversation or returns the existing one (`201` or `200`); `409` without my nickname, `404` for an unknown or blocked person. 10 new a day |
| GET | `/cruising/conversations/:id/messages` | Phase 2. `?after=<ISO>` → `{ items: [{ id, mine, text, createdAt }] }` (the last 100, or newer than `after`); marks them read; `404` when not mine |
| POST | `/cruising/conversations/:id/messages` | Phase 2. `{ text }` (1–1000) → the message; `400` when it shares contact details before each side sent 5. 20/min |
| DELETE | `/cruising/conversations/:id` | Phase 2. `204`: deletes it for both |
| POST | `/cruising/conversations/:id/block` | Phase 2. `204`: deletes it and blocks the person both ways |
| GET | `/cruising/blocks` | Phase 2. `{ items: [{ nickname, createdAt }] }` |
| POST | `/cruising/blocks` | Phase 2. `{ nickname }` → `204`: blocks the person both ways and deletes the conversation with them |
| DELETE | `/cruising/blocks/:nickname` | Phase 2. `204`: lifts my block |
| PUT · GET | `/cruising/winks/:nickname` · `/cruising/winks` | Phase 6. PUT → `204` (one per pair a day, 30 a day, `429` past); GET → winks received `{ items: [{ nickname, verified, at, mutual }] }` |
| GET · PUT | `/cruising/visits` · `/cruising/visits/settings` | Phase 6. My visitors (only with `showVisits` on) / `{ showVisits }` |
| GET · POST | `/cruising/notifications` · `/cruising/notifications/read` | Phase 6. `{ items, unread }` (newest first, 30 days) / `{ ids? }` (none: all) → `204` |
| PUT · DELETE | `/cruising/album-grants/:nickname` | Phase 6. Share or revoke my private album with that member → `204` |
| GET | `/cruising/album-grants` | Phase 6. `{ given: [{ nickname, at }], received: [{ nickname, at }] }` |
| GET · POST | `/cruising/rooms/:countryCode/:citySlug/messages` | Phase 6. `?after=` → `{ items: [{ id, nickname, verified, text, at, mine }] }` (the last 100); POST `{ text }` → the message; `403` muted or account under 24 h; `400` contact details or a link |
| GET | `/cruising/rooms` | Phase 6. My rooms `{ items: [{ countryCode, citySlug, city, lastAt, count24h }] }` |
| GET | `/cruising/suggestions` | Phase 6. `?bbox=` (every edge on the 0.25° grid, else `400`) optional → `{ items }`: up to 12 `CruisingSpotSummary` for onboarding, LIVE only, in the box or else in my country (none set: anywhere), best rated first, then most checked into (`hourCheckIns`). Needs entry. 30/min |
| GET · POST | `/cruising/meetups` | Phase 5. GET `?spotId=&countryCode=&citySlug=` → upcoming meetups `{ id, spot, startsAt, endsAt, title, description, capacity, going, mine, host: { nickname, verified } }`; POST `{ spotId, startsAt, hours, title, description?, capacity? }` → `201` the meetup; `429` past 3 a week |
| PUT · DELETE | `/cruising/meetups/:id/attendance` | Phase 5. PUT `{ anonymous }` → `204` (`409` when full or past); DELETE → `204` |
| DELETE | `/cruising/meetups/:id` | Phase 5. The host cancels: `204`, attendees told |
| GET | `/cruising/spots/:id/hours` | Phase 5. `{ ready, days: number[7][24] }` (`days[0]` is Monday, hours in the spot's local time `timeZone`; weights scaled 0–100), `ready` false under 10 check-ins (and `days` all zeros); needs entry |
| GET · POST · DELETE | `/cruising/trips` · `/cruising/trips/:id` | Phase 5. My trips; POST `{ countryCode, citySlug, from, to, listed }`; `409` past 3 |
| GET | `/cruising/places/:countryCode/:citySlug/visitors` | Phase 5. `{ items: [{ nickname, verified, from, to }] }` listed trips overlapping the next 60 days; needs entry |
| GET · POST | `/cruising/spots/:id/alerts` | Phase 5. Active alerts `{ id, kind, note, createdAt, expiresAt, confirmations, mine }`; POST `{ kind, note? }` → `201`; needs entry, 5 a day |
| POST | `/cruising/alerts/:id/confirm` · `/gone` | Phase 5. `204`; once per member per alert (`409` again, or on my own); `404` once it ended |
| GET | `/cruising/alerts` | Phase 5. `?bbox=` (as `/cruising/active`, on the map's 0.25° grid) → `{ items: [{ spotId, alerts }] }`: LIVE spots in the box with active safety alerts, for the map's warning markers; needs entry |
| PUT · DELETE | `/cruising/reviews/:id/helpful` | Phase 5. `204` (both idempotent); `400` on my own review, `404` for a review that isn't LIVE or whose author is blocked either way. Reviews then carry `helpful: { count, mine }` and their `author` its `badges` (EXPLORER · GUIDE · HELPFUL · LOCAL); `/cruising/members/:nickname` and `/cruising/me`'s profile add `badges` and `contributions: { spotsLive, helpfulReceived, reviewsWritten }`. Guide replaces Explorer |
| GET · PUT | `/cruising/health` | Phase 5. `{ enabled, prepStatus, lastTestedMonth, visible, reminderMonths }` / PUT the same with `consent: true` to enable; `{ enabled: false }` deletes them |
| GET | `/cruising/health-centers` | Phase 5, public. `?countryCode=&citySlug=` or `?bbox=` → centres; `/cruising/health-centers/places` → countries and cities with counts |
| GET · POST · DELETE | `/cruising/trusted-contacts` · `/:id` | Phase 5. Up to 2 `{ id, email (masked), displayName, status: PENDING \| ACCEPTED \| REFUSED }`; POST `{ email, displayName }` sends the invitation |
| POST | `/cruising/trusted-contacts/respond` | Phase 5, public. `{ token, accept }` → `204` (from the invitation email) |
| GET · POST · DELETE | `/cruising/timer` | Phase 5. My active timer; POST `{ minutes (30–360), message?, shareSpot, spotId? }`; PATCH-like `POST /cruising/timer/extend { minutes }`; DELETE ends it |
| POST | `/cruising/verification` | Phase 4. multipart `file` (a photo of the member holding a paper with their nickname, "Cruising" and the date) → `{ status: PENDING }`; `409` without a nickname or when verified/pending; `400` not an image; `413` over 10 MB; `429` past 5 a day |
| GET | `/cruising/verification/file` | Phase 4. My verification photo while it waits (WebP); `404` otherwise |
| GET | `/cruising/media` | Phase 4. My media `{ items: [{ id, ref, kind: PHOTO \| VIDEO, status: PROCESSING \| LIVE \| HIDDEN \| FAILED, cover, position, width, height, durationSeconds, failure, createdAt }], verification: { status, note }, limits: { photos: 12, videos: 4, perDay: 20 } }`; `failure` is a code: `unreadable`, `too-long`, `blocked` or `interrupted` |
| POST | `/cruising/media` | Phase 4. multipart `file` + `consent: true` → the item (`202`, PROCESSING for a video); `409` without a nickname or over 12 photos or 4 videos (FAILED items don't count), `400` no consent, not an image/video, a video over 60 s or a blocked image, `413` a photo over 10 MB or a file over 100 MB, `429` past 20 a day (a deleted item doesn't give its upload back), `507` while the disk is low. The upload is written to disk as it arrives (never held in memory) |
| PATCH | `/cruising/media/:id` | Phase 4. `{ cover?: true, position? }` → `204` |
| DELETE | `/cruising/media/:id` | Phase 4. `204`; the file goes within 6 hours |
| GET | `/cruising/members/:nickname/media` | Phase 4. The member's LIVE media `{ items: [{ id, ref, kind, durationSeconds?, cover }] }`; needs entry; `404` when blocked either way or suspended |
| GET | `/cruising/media/:id/file` · `/poster` | Phase 4. The file (WebP or MP4, range requests) or a video's poster, to its owner, an admin or an entered member who can see it; else `404` |
| GET | `/cruising/search` | `?q=` (2–60) → `{ places: [{ countryCode, city, citySlug, count, bbox: [minLng, minLat, maxLng, maxLat] }], spots: CruisingSpotSummary[] }`, up to 8 each, LIVE only; public. 60/min/IP (section 4.15, phase 3) |
| GET | `/cruising/active` | Phase 3. `?bbox=` (as `/cruising/spots`) → `{ items: [{ spotId, here }] }`: LIVE spots in the box with active check-ins; needs entry |
| GET | `/cruising/favorites` | Phase 3. `{ items: [{ spot: CruisingSpotSummary, here, savedAt }] }`, LIVE spots only, newest first |
| PUT · DELETE | `/cruising/favorites/:spotId` | Phase 3. `204`: saves (LIVE spots only, `409` past 100) or removes a favourite; both idempotent |
| PUT | `/cruising/push` | Phase 3. `{ endpoint, keys: { p256dh, auth } }` → `204`: this device's push subscription (up to 5; the oldest goes); `409` while notifications are off (no VAPID keys) |
| DELETE | `/cruising/push` | Phase 3. `{ endpoint }` → `204` (also when unknown) |
| POST | `/videochat/ticket` | `{ ticket, expiresIn: 30, iceServers: { urls, username?, credential? }[] }`; `409` before onboarding, `403` suspended or age not verified when required, `429` past 10/min per account |
| GET · POST | `/adult/verification` | 4.16 phase 1. `{ status, sourceApp, verifiedAt, note }` (the badge is the account's) / multipart `file` (the member holding a paper with their name, "Codeqlick" and the date) → `{ status: PENDING }`; `409` without a name in the app it comes from or when verified/pending; `400` not an image; `429` past 5 a day. Needs entry in at least one adult app. `GET /adult/verification/file`: my photo while it waits |
| GET · DELETE | `/adult/blocks` · `/adult/blocks/:userRef` | 4.16 phase 1. My blocks in the three apps `{ items: [{ ref, name, sourceApp, createdAt }] }` (`name` in the asking app, null when none) / lifts one in the three → `204`. The per-app block routes create shared blocks |
| GET | `/adult/inbox` | 4.16 phase 2. `?app=&cursor=` → `{ items: [{ app: CRUISING\|SWIPE\|CALL, id, partner: { name, verified, picture: boolean, linked? }, preview, unread, at, call?: { status, durationSec } }], nextCursor }`; only apps entered with the current terms; just the asking app's items when `unifiedInbox` is off |
| GET · PUT | `/adult/preferences` | 4.16 phase 2. `{ unifiedInbox }` |
| GET · POST | `/adult/notifications` · `/adult/notifications/read` | 4.16 phase 2. `?app=` → `{ items: [{ id, app, kind, data, readAt, createdAt }], unread }` (newest first, 30 days) / `{ ids? }` (none: all) → `204`. `GET /adult/notifications/unread` → `{ unread }` for the bell |
| PUT · DELETE | `/adult/push` | 4.16 phase 2. `{ app, endpoint, keys: { p256dh, auth }, crossApp? }` → `204` (up to 5 per app; the oldest goes) / `{ endpoint }` → `204`. `/cruising/push`, `/swipe/push` and `/videochat/push` answer the same for one release |
| POST | `/adult/calls` | 4.16 phase 3. `{ app: CRUISING\|SWIPE, conversationId }` → `201 { id, status: RINGING, expiresAt }`; `409 videochat-entry` when the caller hasn't entered Videochat (the web takes them there), `409` without a message from each side (Cruising) or with a call ringing, `403` blocked or suspended, `429` over the limits |
| POST · DELETE | `/adult/calls/:id/answer` · `/adult/calls/:id` | 4.16 phase 3. `{ accept }` → `{ status, callUrl? }` (`callUrl` the Videochat page; `409 videochat-entry` when the callee must enter Videochat first, keeping the call 5 minutes) / the caller cancels → `204` |
| GET | `/adult/calls/:id` | 4.16 phase 3. `{ id, app, status, partner: { name, verified }, createdAt, answeredAt, endedAt, durationSec }` to either party; `404` otherwise |
| GET · PUT · DELETE | `/adult/link` | 4.16 phase 4. `{ apps, consentAt, photo: { app, mediaId } \| null, name }` / `{ apps (≥ 2 entered apps), consent: true }` → `204` (links or changes the apps) / unlinks everything at once → `204` |
| PUT | `/adult/link/name` | 4.16 phase 4. `{ name }` (3–20 of `a-z0-9_`) → `204`: sets the username and the Cruising nickname together; `400` invalid or reserved, `409` taken or held by someone else; `409` when Cruising isn't linked |
| PUT · DELETE | `/adult/link/photo` | 4.16 phase 4. `{ app, mediaId }` (a LIVE public photo of mine in a linked app) → `204` / `204` |

### Client portal (role `CLIENT`; admins may also call)

| Method | Path | Response |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/portal/projects` | `ClientProject[]` owned by the caller, each with its `milestones[]` |
| GET | `/portal/todo` | What waits on the client, oldest first: `{ items: [{ kind, id, title, projectId, projectName, since }] }`, `kind` being `approval` (an update asking for approval, `id` the update's), `proposal` (sent, not expired), `ticket` (a request `WAITING_CLIENT`, `title` with its `#number`), `brief` (questionnaire requested, not sent; `id` the project's) `review` (review requested; `id` the project's) or `invoice` (a team invoice not paid; `id` the invoice's, `title` its number, `since` its `issuedOn`) |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id` | `ClientProject` with `updates[]` and `files[]` (`{ id, name, contentType, size, createdAt }`), newest first, and `milestones[]` (`{ id, title, description, dueDate, completedAt }`) in the admin's order; 404 if not the caller's |
| POST | `/portal/projects/:id/updates/:updateId/comments` | `{ body }` → `201 UpdateComment` (`{ id, body, createdAt, author: { name, team } }`). A client's comment emails the team inbox (`LEADS_NOTIFY_TO`, reply-to the client) |
| POST | `/portal/projects/:id/updates/:updateId/approval` | Role CLIENT only (403 for admins) → `ProjectUpdate` with `approvedAt`; 400 when the update doesn't ask for approval; approving again keeps the first approval. Emails the team inbox |
| GET / PUT | `/portal/projects/:id/brief` | `{ status: 'NONE' | 'REQUESTED' | 'SUBMITTED', answers, requestedAt, submittedAt, updatedAt }` / `{ answers, submit? }`: saves; with `submit`, sends it (400 listing `answers.<key>` when an essential one is missing) and emails the team inbox |
| GET / PUT | `/portal/projects/:id/review` | The review (`status: 'NONE'` when not asked for) / `{ rating, quote, authorName, authorRole?, consent, locale }` (400 when not asked for); emails the team inbox. Sending again puts a published one back to review |
| GET / POST | `/portal/projects/:id/credentials` | List (`{ id, label, url, username, createdBy: { name }, createdAt, viewedAt }`, never the secret) / `{ label, url?, username?, secret, notes? }` → `201` |
| DELETE | `/portal/projects/:id/credentials/:credentialId` | `204` |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id/status` | `SiteMonitor` view (`{ status, statusSince, uptime30, days: [{ day, checks, ratio }] (30 Madrid days), certExpiresAt, lastCheckedAt, ... }`); 404 unless the project is the caller's and monitored |
| GET / PUT | `/portal/projects/:id/site` | The client's managed website: `{ status, preset, url, previewUrl, domain: { name, state, checkedAt, mail, nicHandle, serverIp }, lastBuildAt, businessName, colors: { primary, primaryText }, editable, unpublishedSince, legalMissing, menu: { enabled, published }, english }` (`english`: the published site has its English version, decision 106; `unpublishedSince`: when the editor's draft was saved, if it differs from the published site, else null; `legalMissing`: `taxId`/`address` the published legal data lacks; `nicHandle` and `serverIp` are what the client sets at their registrar), `editable` being what they may change (`tagline`, `contact` — with `reviewUrl`, the Google review link of section 8.2 —, `hours`, `hero: { title, text }`, `services`, `about: { title, paragraphs }`, `gallery`, `testimonials`; brand, theme and legal data stay with the team) / `{ site: editable }` → merged into the content, validated as a whole (400 with the field in the message), saved as a change by the client (a revision) and published; the team inbox is emailed what changed (or that publishing failed). Changing the hours drops the machine-readable `openingHoursSpec`. 404 unless the project is the caller's and has a managed site. Limited to 30/hour |
| PUT | `/portal/projects/:id/site/domain` | `{ name: string \| null }` → the site: the client chooses, changes or drops their own domain; it is rebuilt and the domain checked right away. 400 for a Codeqlick domain, 409 when another site uses it. Limited to 10/hour |
| GET / PUT / DELETE | `/portal/projects/:id/site/draft` | The website editor (section 8.2): `{ content, preset, presets: [{ key, theme, images }], published: { content, preset }, draft: { updatedAt, previewUrl, previewStatus } \| null, bookingPages: [{ slug, title, url, enabled }], menuEnabled, newsEnabled }` (`content`/`preset`: the draft's, or the published ones without a draft; `bookingPages`: the Citas pages of the people of the client account, for the site's booking button; the content may carry `english` and `en`, the English version of section 8.2, validated like the rest) / `{ content, preset?, rebuild? }` → the same: saved as the draft (400 with every issue as `{ path, message }`, nothing saved), its preview built when `rebuild` (or when there was none) / `204`: the draft is discarded and its preview deleted. Limited to 600/hour |
| POST | `/portal/projects/:id/site/draft/publish` | The draft becomes the site's content (a revision of the previous one) and is published; the team inbox is emailed what changed. 409 without a draft. Limited to 30/hour |
| POST | `/portal/projects/:id/site/draft/style` | `{ preset }` → the editor view: the draft takes that style's colours, fonts, corners, section order and photos (uploaded photos stay), keeping every text |
| GET | `/site-drafts/:key/*` | Public (the key is the secret): a file of the draft's preview, streamed from the builder; 404 when it doesn't exist or was discarded |
| POST | `/portal/projects/:id/site/images` | Multipart `file` (JPEG, PNG or WebP, 5 MB) → `201 { file: 'media/<name>', url }`, stored with the uploaded images (an image a managed site uses counts as used in the media library). Limited to 20/hour |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id/visits` | `?days=7\|30\|90` (default 30) → `{ enabled, available, days, visitors, pageviews, messages, daily: [{ date, visitors, pageviews, messages }], referrers: [{ source, visitors }] (5) }`: the managed site's numbers from Umami and its form's messages; `enabled: false` until it has statistics. Also `GET /admin/projects/:id/visits`. 404 without a managed site of the caller's |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id/maintenance` | The project's maintenance plan (section 8.2, decision 110) → `MaintenanceView` (shape below). 404 unless the project is the caller's and has a managed site |
| POST | `/portal/projects/:id/maintenance/checkout` | `{ locale }` → `{ url }`: a Stripe Checkout Session (subscription mode, metadata `kind=site-maintenance` and `projectId`). Role CLIENT only (403 for admins); 404 while the plan is off or without a managed site; 409 when the plan is already on. Limited to 10/min |
| POST | `/portal/projects/:id/maintenance/portal` | `{ locale }` → `{ url }`: Stripe's customer portal for the project's customer, back to "Tu web". Role CLIENT only; 404 while Stripe is off; 409 when the project has no Stripe customer. Limited to 10/min |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id/site/messages` | `?page&pageSize` → `Paginated<{ id, name, email, phone, message, createdAt }>`: the messages the site's form kept (12 months), newest first. 404 without a managed site of the caller's |
| DELETE | `/portal/projects/:id/site/messages/:messageId` | `204`: the client deletes a message (for example when the visitor asks); 404 if it isn't one of that site's |
| GET / POST | `/portal/projects/:id/site/news` | News of the managed site (section 8.2): GET `?status=DRAFT\|PUBLISHED\|EXPIRED&page&pageSize` → `Paginated<SiteNewsPost>` (shape below), newest first, with `enabled` (the switch) beside it / `{ title, summary, body, titleEn?, summaryEn?, bodyEn?, photo?, expiresAt? (YYYY-MM-DD) }` → `201 SiteNewsPost` as a draft (400 with the field in the message, or past 200 posts; 400 when `photo` isn't a file of the media library, i.e. one uploaded through `/site/images`). Nothing is built. 404 unless the project is the caller's and has a managed site. Writes limited to 60/hour |
| GET / PATCH / DELETE | `/portal/projects/:id/site/news/:postId` | `SiteNewsPost` / the same fields, all optional (the slug never changes; `photo: null`, `expiresAt: null` and `titleEn`/`summaryEn`/`bodyEn: null` remove them) → `SiteNewsPost`; the site is rebuilt when the post is published / `204`, the site rebuilt when the post was published. 404 if it isn't one of that site's |
| POST | `/portal/projects/:id/site/news/:postId/publish` · `/unpublish` | → `SiteNewsPost`: publishing sets `publishedAt` (kept on later edits) and rebuilds the site (409 when `expiresAt` is already past); unpublishing clears it and rebuilds. The answer carries the build's result as `build: 'OK' \| 'FAILED'`; 503 when the builder isn't configured, with the post unchanged. Limited to 30/hour |
| PUT | `/portal/projects/:id/site/news-settings` | `{ enabled }` → `{ enabled }`: switches the section on or off; the site is rebuilt. Limited to 30/hour |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id/tasks` | The project's tasks without `description` (`{ id, title, status, ticket, doneAt, createdAt, updatedAt }`) |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id/plan` | `{ active, name, monthlyMinutes, alertPercent, month, usedMinutes, remainingMinutes, percent, months: [{ month, minutes }] (4, newest first) }`; 404 unless the project is the caller's and the plan is active |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id/time` | `ProjectTime` (`{ totalMinutes, months: [{ month: 'yyyy-mm', minutes }], entries: TimeEntry[] }`, newest first); 404 unless the project is the caller's and has `showHours` |
| GET | `/portal/projects/:id/files/:fileId` | The file, always as `application/octet-stream` with `Content-Disposition: attachment` (UTF-8 name); 404 if the project isn't the caller's |
| GET | `/portal/invoices` | Every invoice of the caller's account, newest first: `{ items: Invoice[] }` (shape below), the team's (`source: 'team'`) and those Stripe issued for websites bought online (`source: 'stripe'`, decision 20) |
| GET | `/portal/invoices/:id/file` | A team invoice's PDF, as an `application/pdf` attachment; 404 if it isn't the caller's |
| GET | `/portal/invoices/stripe/:purchaseId` | `{ url }`: the PDF of the invoice Stripe issued for that purchase, asked to Stripe at that moment (its links don't last); 404 if the purchase isn't of one of the caller's projects or has no invoice |
| GET | `/portal/proposals` | `?status&page&pageSize` → `Paginated<Proposal>` of the caller's proposals, drafts left out |
| GET | `/portal/proposals/:id` | `Proposal`; 404 for a draft or someone else's |
| POST | `/portal/proposals/:id/accept` | Role CLIENT only (403 for admins). `{ name }` → `Proposal` `ACCEPTED`; 409 unless `SENT` and not expired. Emails the team inbox |
| POST | `/portal/proposals/:id/decline` | Role CLIENT only. `{ reason? }` → `Proposal` `DECLINED`; 409 unless `SENT`. Emails the team inbox |
| GET / POST | `/portal/tickets` | GET `?page&pageSize` → `Paginated<Ticket>` of the caller's requests, latest activity first. POST (role CLIENT only) `{ type, priority?, title, body, projectId? }` → `201 TicketDetail`; 400 when `projectId` isn't one of the caller's projects. Emails the team inbox (`LEADS_NOTIFY_TO`, reply-to the client) |
| GET | `/portal/tickets/:id` | `TicketDetail`; 404 if it isn't the caller's |
| POST | `/portal/tickets/:id/messages` | `{ body }` → `TicketDetail`. A request `WAITING_CLIENT` or `RESOLVED` goes back to `IN_PROGRESS`; 409 when it is `CLOSED`. Emails the team inbox |
| POST | `/portal/tickets/:id/close` | → `TicketDetail` with status `CLOSED` (no more replies) |
| POST | `/portal/tickets/:id/files` | Multipart, one `file` field, up to 25 MB; the request's client or an admin → `201 TicketDetail`; 409 when `CLOSED`. A client's file emails the team inbox |
| GET | `/portal/tickets/:id/files/:fileId` | The file, always as `application/octet-stream` attachment; 404 if the request isn't the caller's (admins may read any) |

### Cloud (section 4.13; a session or, where marked 🔑, an API token)

Team routes take the team's `slug`; project routes the project's `id`. Not a member: `404`. A role too low: `403`. A token restricted to another team: `404`. Every change is a `CloudEvent`.

| Method | Path | Body / query | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/cloud/cli/device` | Public. `{ clientName }` (1–60, the machine's name) | `{ deviceCode, userCode, verificationUri, verificationUriComplete, expiresIn: 600, interval: 5 }`. 10/min/IP |
| POST | `/cloud/cli/device/token` | Public. `{ deviceCode }` | `{ status: 'pending' \| 'denied' \| 'expired' }` or, once, `{ status: 'approved', token, user: { id, name, email } }`; polling faster than every 5 s answers `429` |
| GET | `/cloud/cli/device/:userCode` | | `{ userCode, clientName, createdAt, expiresAt, status }`; `404` unknown or expired |
| POST | `/cloud/cli/device/:userCode` | `{ approve: boolean }` | `204` |
| GET | `/cloud/me` 🔑 | | `{ user: { id, name, email }, via: 'session' \| 'token', token: { id, name, teamId } \| null, teams: [{ id, slug, name, role, plan, requireTwoFactor, twoFactorBlocked }], termsVersion, termsAccepted }` (4.13 Terms) |
| POST | `/cloud/terms/accept` | `{ acceptTerms: true, termsVersion }` (session only) | `204`: every membership of the caller now has the current terms; `409` for an old `termsVersion` |
| GET | `/cloud/tokens` | | `{ items: [{ id, name, prefix, team: { slug, name } \| null, readOnly, expiresAt, lastUsedAt, lastUsedIp, createdAt }] }` (mine) |
| POST | `/cloud/tokens` | `{ name, teamSlug?, expiresInDays: 30 \| 90 \| 365 \| null, readOnly?: boolean }` (`readOnly` default false) | `201 { token, item }` (`token` shown once; `item` as in the list); `409` past 20 |
| DELETE | `/cloud/tokens/:id` | | `204` |
| POST | `/cloud/teams` | `{ name, slug, acceptTerms: true, termsVersion }` | `201` the team; `409` slug taken or past 3 owned / 10 joined |
| GET / PATCH / DELETE | `/cloud/teams/:team` | PATCH `{ name?, requireTwoFactor? }` (ADMIN; `requireTwoFactor` OWNER) | `{ id, slug, name, plan, role, requireTwoFactor, twoFactorBlocked, createdAt }` / the team / `204` (OWNER; its projects deleted). PATCH: `409` turning `requireTwoFactor` on without two-step verification on your own account. Every team route: `403` while the team requires two-step verification and your account has none (section 4.13) |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/members` | | `{ items: [{ userId, name, email, role, twoFactor, joinedAt }], invites: [{ id, email, role, expiresAt }] }` |
| POST | `/cloud/teams/:team/invites` | ADMIN. `{ email, role: 'ADMIN' \| 'MEMBER', locale }` | `201` the invite (emailed); `409` already a member, invited, or 10 members |
| DELETE | `/cloud/teams/:team/invites/:id` | ADMIN | `204` |
| GET | `/cloud/invites/:token` | | `{ team: { name, slug, requireTwoFactor }, role, invitedBy, email, expiresAt, emailMatches }`; `404` unknown, expired or used |
| POST | `/cloud/invites/:token/accept` | `{ acceptTerms: true, termsVersion }` | `{ team }`; `403` when my email isn't the invited one |
| PATCH | `/cloud/teams/:team/members/:userId` | `{ role }` (OWNER for owners; ADMIN otherwise) | `204`; `409` would leave no owner |
| DELETE | `/cloud/teams/:team/members/:userId` | ADMIN, or myself to leave | `204`; `409` the last owner |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/usage` 🔑 | | `{ plan, limits, projects, storageBytes, deploymentsLastHour, members, meters }`; `meters` (usage alerts) is `[{ key: 'builds' \| 'formSubmissions' \| 'analyticsEvents' \| 'storage' \| 'deploymentsPerHour', used, limit, percent }]`, `percent` being `floor(used / limit × 100)` (may pass 100); the console shows its banner from it |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/events` 🔑 | `?page&pageSize&from&to&action` (filters as in the export) | `Paginated<CloudEvent>` newest first |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/events.csv` 🔑 | ADMIN. `?from&to&action` (dates `YYYY-MM-DD`, at most 365 days apart, default the last 30 days; `action` exact or a prefix ending in `.`) | `text/csv` attachment `<team>-events-<from>-<to>.csv`, streamed, newest first; `400` bad dates or range, `429` past 10/h per IP (section 4.13, Events) |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/projects` 🔑 | | `{ items: CloudProject[] }` |
| POST | `/cloud/teams/:team/projects` 🔑 | ADMIN. `{ name, slug }` | `201 CloudProject`; `409` slug taken or reserved, or past 10 |
| GET / PATCH / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id` 🔑 | PATCH `{ name?, productionChecks? }` (ADMIN; at least one; `productionChecks` is the weekly check of production, decision 81: team events `project.production_checks_enabled` / `project.production_checks_disabled`; `project.renamed` only for the name) | `CloudProject` / `CloudProject` / `204` (ADMIN, see 4.13) |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/transfer` | Session only (it moves data between teams, like member management). OWNER of the project's team and OWNER or ADMIN of the target. `{ team }` (the target's slug) | `200 CloudProject` in the target team with `git: 'kept' \| 'dropped' \| 'none'`; `400` same team, `403` role or suspended, `404` project or team, `409` a build queued or running, a limit of the target team (projects, storage, monitors) or the project changed meanwhile (see 4.13, Teams and projects) |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/deployments` 🔑 | `?page&pageSize` | `Paginated<CloudDeployment>` newest first |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/deployments` 🔑 | Multipart: `bundle` (tar.gz), `target` (`preview` \| `production`), `message?` (≤ 200), `commit?` (hex, ≤ 40), `branch?` (≤ 100), `config?` (the text of `codeqlick.json`, 4.13) | `201 CloudDeployment`; `400` with the reason (an invalid `config` names the path and lists `errors: [{ path, message }]`), `403` suspended, `413` too big, `429` past 30/h |
| GET / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/deployments/:deploymentId` 🔑 | | `CloudDeployment` / `204` (`409` when production) |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/deployments/:deploymentId/files` 🔑 | | `{ items: [{ path, size }], count, totalBytes }` sorted by path (first 10,000); `404` unknown or files gone. 30/min |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/deployments/:deploymentId/compare` 🔑 | `?with=production\|<deploymentId>` (default `production`) | `{ deploymentId, withId, added: [{ path, size }], removed: [{ path, size }], changed: [{ path, size, previousSize }], unchanged, totalBytes, withTotalBytes }`; `404` either unknown (or not this project's) or files gone, `409` no production. 10/min |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/deployments/:deploymentId/promote` 🔑 | | `CloudProject` with it as production; `403` suspended |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/deployments/:deploymentId/archive` 🔑 | | `application/gzip` stream `<slug>-<shortId>.tar.gz` (event `deployment.downloaded`); `404` deleted or its files gone, `429` past 10/h per team, `503` hosting not set up |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/production/unlock` 🔑 | ADMIN | `CloudProject` (unchanged and `200` when it wasn't locked) |
| POST / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/deployments/:deploymentId/keep` 🔑 | | `CloudDeployment` kept / not kept (`200`; member role; `404` unknown, `409` past 5 kept on POST) |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/domains` 🔑 | | `{ items: CloudDomain[] }` |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/domains` 🔑 | `{ name }` | `201 CloudDomain` (with what to add to the DNS); `400` not allowed, `409` taken or past 5 |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/domains/:domainId/check` 🔑 | | `CloudDomain` checked now. 10/min |
| DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/domains/:domainId` 🔑 | | `204` |
| GET / PUT | `/cloud/projects/:id/protection` 🔑 | PUT `{ mode: 'OFF' \| 'PREVIEWS' \| 'ALL', password? (8–128), days?: 1 \| 7 \| 30 }` (ADMIN) | `CloudProtection`; `400` invalid or turning it on without a password, `503` without `CLOUD_PROTECTION_KEY` (turning it on). The `GET` answer also carries `bypass` |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/protection/bypass` 🔑 | | `200 { token, prefix, createdAt }` (the token only in this answer): creates or replaces the bypass token for automation (ADMIN; read-only tokens refused; `403` suspended; `503` without `CLOUD_PROTECTION_KEY`; 10/min) |
| DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/protection/bypass` 🔑 | | `204` revokes it (ADMIN; also `204` when there is none, and then no event is recorded) |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/protection/ticket` 🔑 | `?host` (one of the project's addresses) | `{ url, expiresIn: 60 }`: `https://<host>/__codeqlick/enter?ticket=…`, or the plain `https://<host>/` when that address isn't protected; `400` not one of the project's addresses |
| GET | `/cloud/schema/codeqlick.json` | Public, cached a day | The JSON Schema of `codeqlick.json` (`application/schema+json`, CORS for any origin) |
| POST | `/cloud/config/check` 🔑 | Multipart (a small JSON body works too) with fields `teamSlug` and `config` (the file's text, as in the deployment upload; any member) | `{ ok: true, summary: { redirects, rewrites, headers, cleanUrls, trailingSlash } }` or `{ ok: false, errors: [{ path, message }] }` (both `200`; limits of the team's plan; nothing is kept). `400` when `config` isn't a string or is over 1 MB (the multipart field takes up to 2 MB, so the answer is a `400`, not a `413`) |
| GET | `/cloud/edge/check` | Nginx only (`X-Cq-Protection-Key`), one call per request that needs it (password protection and `codeqlick.json` rules, 4.13). Headers `X-Cq-Host`, `X-Cq-Project`, `X-Cq-Kind` (`previews` \| `production`), `X-Cq-Access`, `X-Cq-Bypass` (the visitor's `X-Codeqlick-Bypass`, when sent), `X-Cq-Deployment` (id), `X-Cq-Uri` (the request's decoded path), `X-Cq-Args` (its query string) | `204` serve, with `X-Cq-Path` (the file path to serve; `/.cq-none` when no file answers; empty when the deployment has no rules) and `X-Cq-H-<name>` for each header to add; `401` ask for the password; `403` redirect, with `X-Cq-Status` (`301` \| `302` \| `307` \| `308`) and `X-Cq-Location`; `403` without `X-Cq-Status` = wrong key. Never reachable from api.codeqlick.com |
| GET | `/cloud/protection/check` | Deprecated alias of `GET /cloud/edge/check` (same handler and answers), kept for one release so Nginx snippets that predate it keep working; removed afterwards | as `/cloud/edge/check` |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/protection/password` | Nginx only, as `/__codeqlick/password` on the hosting address. GET `?next`; POST form `password`, `next` | The password page (HTML, EN/ES); POST right: `303` to `next` with the cookie, wrong: the page with the error (`401`), `429` past 5/min per IP and project |
| GET | `/cloud/protection/enter` | Nginx only, as `/__codeqlick/enter`. `?ticket&next` | `303` to `next` (a safe same-host path, default `/`) with the cookie; the password page when the ticket is invalid |
| GET | `/cloud/protection/comments` | Nginx only, as `/__codeqlick/comments` on a deployment address. `?path` | The preview comments page (HTML); `404` on production addresses and custom domains (decision 111) |
| GET | `/cloud/comments/overlay.js` (also `/cloud/protection/comments/overlay.js`) | Public | The comments overlay script (`Cache-Control: public, max-age=300`) |
| GET | `/cloud/protection/comments/api/me` | Nginx only; `X-Codeqlick-Comments` token | `{ user, project, deployment, members, inboxUrl, expiresAt }`; `401` invalid or expired token, `404` production |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/protection/comments/api/threads` | Nginx only; token. POST `{ path, x, y, selector?, viewportWidth?, body, mentions? }` | The deployment's threads / `201` the thread; `409` past the limits, `429` |
| POST | `/cloud/protection/comments/api/threads/:threadId/comments` | Nginx only; token. `{ body, mentions? }` | `201` the comment |
| PATCH | `/cloud/protection/comments/api/threads/:threadId` | Nginx only; token. `{ resolved }` | The thread |
| DELETE | `/cloud/protection/comments/api/threads/:threadId/comments/:commentId` | Nginx only; token | `204`; `403` not the author nor an admin |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/comments` 🔑 | `?status=open\|resolved\|all&deploymentId&path` | `{ open, items: CloudCommentThread[] }` (each with `comments`) |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/comments/session` | Session only. `{ deploymentId, path? }` | `{ url, expiresAt }`: the deployment's comments page with a 2-hour token (through the members' ticket when previews are protected); `401` with an API token |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/comments/:threadId/comments` 🔑 | `{ body, mentions? }` | `201` the reply |
| PATCH | `/cloud/projects/:id/comments/:threadId` 🔑 | `{ resolved }` | The thread |
| DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/comments/:threadId/comments/:commentId` 🔑 | | `204`; `403` not the author nor an admin |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/forms` 🔑 | POST `{ name, notify, recipientIds, spamFilter? }` (ADMIN; `spamFilter` default true) | `{ items: CloudForm[] }` / `201 CloudForm`; `409` past the plan's forms |
| PATCH / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/forms/:formId` 🔑 | PATCH `{ name?, enabled?, notify?, recipientIds?, spamFilter? }` (ADMIN) | `CloudForm` / `204` (its submissions too) |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/forms/:formId/key` 🔑 | ADMIN | `CloudForm` with a new key |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/forms/:formId/submissions` 🔑 | `?q&page&pageSize&spam` (`spam=true` lists spam, default `false`) | `Paginated<{ id, data, email, page, spam, spamReason, createdAt }>` newest first |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/forms/:formId/submissions.csv` 🔑 | | `text/csv` |
| DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/forms/:formId/submissions/:submissionId` 🔑 | | `204` |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/forms/:formId/submissions/:submissionId/spam` 🔑 | `{ spam }` (MEMBER) | The submission; `404` unknown |
| POST | `/cloud/forms/:key` | Public. The form's fields (see Forms) | JSON `{ ok }`, a `303` back to the site, or a short page; `400` invalid or with files, `403` other origin, `404` unknown or off, `429` |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/teams/:team/monitors` 🔑 | POST `{ name, kind, url?, keyword?, keywordMode? \| periodMinutes?, graceMinutes?, projectId?, alertIds, slowMs? }` (ADMIN; `keywordMode` defaults to `CONTAINS`) | `{ items: CloudMonitor[] }` / `201 CloudMonitor` (heartbeats with their URL); `400` an unsafe URL, `slowMs` not an integer from 500 to 14000 (or `null`) or set on a heartbeat, `409` past 10 |
| GET / PATCH / DELETE | `/cloud/monitors/:id` 🔑 | PATCH `{ name?, enabled?, url?, keyword? (null clears it), keywordMode?, periodMinutes?, graceMinutes?, alertIds?, slowMs? }` (ADMIN) | `CloudMonitor` with `checks` (last 24 h), `uptime30`, `days` (90), `incidents` (30 days) and `responseStats` (24 h, 7 d and 30 d: `{ checks, median, p95, average } \| null` each, in ms; `null` for heartbeats) / `CloudMonitor` / `204` |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/heartbeats/:key` | Public; `?status=fail` or `{ "status": "fail" }` | `200 { ok: true }`; `404` unknown or paused. 30/min per key |
| GET / PUT | `/cloud/teams/:team/status-page` 🔑 | PUT `{ enabled, title, intro?, items: [{ monitorId, label }], subscriptions? }` (ADMIN; `subscriptions` omitted keeps it) | The page's settings, with `subscriptions` |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/status-subscribers` 🔑 | `?status=PENDING\|ACTIVE\|UNSUBSCRIBED&page&pageSize` (ADMIN) | `{ counts: { active, pending, unsubscribed }, limit, items: Paginated<CloudStatusSubscriberView> }` newest first; emails masked |
| DELETE | `/cloud/teams/:team/status-subscribers` 🔑 | (ADMIN) | `{ deleted }`: every subscriber of the team erased. Team event `status_subscribers.deleted_all` |
| DELETE | `/cloud/teams/:team/status-subscribers/:id` 🔑 | (ADMIN) | `204`; `404` unknown or of another team. Team event `status_subscriber.deleted` (id only) |
| POST | `/cloud/status/:team/subscribers` | Public. `{ email, locale, acceptPrivacy: true, website? (honeypot) }` (4.13 "Email subscriptions") | `202` always for an address. `400` field error `email: disposable`; `404` without an enabled page or with subscriptions off; `409` at the plan's cap; `429` past 60 an hour for the team. A new, `PENDING` or `UNSUBSCRIBED` address is emailed a link to `CLOUD_URL/{locale}/status-updates/confirm?token=…` (48 hours; at most one per address and team every 10 minutes, 5 a day per address over all teams); an `ACTIVE` one is sent nothing — limited to 5/min/IP |
| POST | `/cloud/status-subscribers/confirm` | Public. `{ token }` | `200 { teamName, teamSlug, locale }`: the subscriber is `ACTIVE`. `400` unknown or expired token — limited to 10/min/IP |
| POST | `/cloud/status-subscribers/unsubscribe` | Public. `{ token }` (the subscriber's `unsubscribeToken`) | `200 { teamName, teamSlug, locale }`, also when already unsubscribed; `404` unknown token — limited to 10/min/IP |
| POST | `/cloud/status-subscribers/unsubscribe/:token` | One-click unsubscribe (RFC 8058), body `List-Unsubscribe=One-Click` (ignored); the address of the emails' `List-Unsubscribe` header | `200`, same effect; `404` unknown token — limited to 60/min/IP |
| GET | `/cloud/status-subscribers/unsubscribe/:token` | For mail clients that open the header's address in a browser | `302` to `CLOUD_URL/{locale}/status-updates/unsubscribe?token=…` (the subscriber's language; `en` for an unknown token); changes nothing — limited to 30/min/IP |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/teams/:team/status-notices` 🔑 | GET `?open=true\|false&page&pageSize`; POST `{ kind, title, monitorIds, body, state?, startsAt?, endsAt? }` (ADMIN; `state` for incidents, default `INVESTIGATING`; `startsAt` and `endsAt` required for maintenance) | GET `Paginated<CloudStatusNotice>` with `updates`, newest first; POST `201 CloudStatusNotice`; `400` invalid window or monitors not of the team, `409` past 20 open |
| GET / PATCH / DELETE | `/cloud/status-notices/:id` 🔑 | PATCH `{ title?, monitorIds?, startsAt?, endsAt? }` (ADMIN; times only for a maintenance not completed) | The notice / `204`; `400` invalid window or monitors, `409` a completed maintenance |
| POST | `/cloud/status-notices/:id/updates` 🔑 | `{ body, state? }` (ADMIN) | `201 CloudStatusNotice`; `400` a state not allowed for its kind, `409` past 50 updates or a completed maintenance |
| GET | `/cloud/status/:team` | Public, cached 60 s | `{ title, intro, overall, teamName, subscribe (true while email subscriptions are on and the plan's cap has room), monitors: [{ label, status, uptime90, days, incidents }], notices: [{ id, kind, title, state, monitors: [label], startsAt, endsAt, resolvedAt, createdAt, updates: [{ state, body, createdAt }] }] }` (open notices and those closed in the last 14 days); a monitor's `status` may be `MAINTENANCE` and `overall` may be `maintenance`; `404` without an enabled page |
| GET | `/cloud/status/:team/badge.svg` | Public, cached 60 s | `image/svg+xml` |
| GET | `/cloud/badges/:slug.svg` | Public, cached 60 s; limited to 600/min/IP (GitHub fetches README images from a few shared addresses) | `image/svg+xml`: the project's deployment state (4.13 "Status badge"); the gray `unknown` for a project that doesn't exist or is suspended |
| POST | `/cloud/audits` 🔑 | `{ url, teamSlug, locale? }` | The report (see Website check API); `422` couldn't open it, `429` past 20/h, `504` over 60 s |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/audits` 🔑 | `?page&pageSize` | `Paginated<{ id, url, score, kind, createdAt }>` (`kind`: `'manual' \| 'preview' \| 'scheduled'`, decisions 69 and 81; replaces the former boolean `preview`) |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/production-checks` 🔑 | Any member | `{ enabled, nextAt: string \| null, url, items: [{ id, score, categories: { speed, mobile, seo, security }, createdAt, dropped: boolean }] }`: the last 12 scheduled checks, newest first; `dropped` is the Comparison rule of 4.13 against the check before it (the 12th is compared with the 13th, which isn't listed); `nextAt` null when off |
| GET | `/cloud/audits/:id` 🔑 | `?locale` | The report |
| GET | `/cloud/github/install-url` | `?teamSlug` (ADMIN) | `{ url }`: the app's installation page with a signed state; `503` when the app isn't set up |
| POST | `/cloud/github/installations` | `{ installationId, code, state }` | `{ installation: { id, accountLogin } }` after checking with the person's GitHub token; `403` when they can't see it, `409` linked to another team |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/github` 🔑 | | `{ configured, installations: [{ id, accountLogin, accountType }] }` |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/github/repos` 🔑 | `?installationId` | `{ items: [{ id, fullName, defaultBranch, private }] }` (the installation's repositories) |
| PUT | `/cloud/projects/:id/rebuild-schedule` 🔑 | `{ frequency: 'off' \| 'hourly' \| 'daily' \| 'weekly', minute?, hour?, weekday? }` (ADMIN; `minute` for all but `off`, `hour` for daily and weekly, `weekday` for weekly) | `CloudProject` with `rebuild: { frequency, minute, hour, weekday, nextAt, failures }`; `400` missing fields, `409` not connected to Git |
| GET | `/cloud/teams/:team/github/detect` 🔑 | `?installationId&repoId&branch&rootDir` (ADMIN; 20/min per address) | `{ framework: 'astro' \| 'docusaurus' \| 'gatsby' \| 'next' \| 'nuxt' \| 'sveltekit' \| 'eleventy' \| 'cra' \| 'vite' \| null, buildCommand, outputDir, nodeVersion, nodeFrom: '.nvmrc' \| '.node-version' \| 'engines' \| null, notes: ('next-not-static' \| 'sveltekit-no-static-adapter' \| 'node-unsupported' \| 'no-package-json')[] }` (4.13, Suggested settings); `404` unknown repository, branch or folder; `503` without the GitHub App or while GitHub limits us (its 403 or 429) |
| PUT / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/git` 🔑 | PUT `{ installationId, repoId, branch, rootDir?, installCommand?, buildCommand?, outputDir?, nodeVersion?, buildBranches?, buildPullRequests?, previewChecks?, onlyRootChanges? }` (ADMIN) | `CloudProject` with its `git`; DELETE disconnects |
| GET / PUT / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/env` 🔑 | PUT `{ key, value, production, preview, branch? }` (ADMIN; with `branch`, `production` must be false and `preview` true, else `400`); DELETE `?key&branch` (without `branch`: the general one) | `{ items: [{ key, production, preview, branch: string \| null, updatedAt }] }` (never values; by key, the general one first, then branches A–Z) |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/env/import` 🔑 | ADMIN. `{ content (≤ 256 KB, dotenv), production, preview, overwrite }` | `{ created, updated, skipped }` (keys only); `400` `{ errors: [{ line, key?, message }] }` and nothing saved when any line is wrong, the file has no variables, or the project would pass its plan's variables (section 4.13) |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/builds` 🔑 | GET `?page`; POST `{ branch?, noCache? }` (a build of the branch's latest commit; `noCache: true` runs it without the dependency cache and is stored on the build) | `Paginated<CloudBuild>` (each with `audit`) / `201 CloudBuild`; `409` without a repository, `429` past the month's builds |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/builds/:buildId` 🔑 | `?after` (log offset) | The build (with `audit`) and its log (from `after`) |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/builds/:buildId/log` 🔑 | | `text/plain; charset=utf-8` attachment `<project-slug>-build-<number>.log`, `Cache-Control: no-store`: the whole kept log (at most 1 MB; empty once cleared after 30 days; what is there so far while running); `404` as for the build above |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/builds/:buildId/cancel` 🔑 | | The build (`CANCELED`; a running one is stopped by the agent at its next heartbeat) |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/hooks` 🔑 | POST `{ name (1–60), branch? }` (ADMIN; `branch` defaults to the production branch) | `{ items: CloudDeployHook[] }` (members) / `201 CloudDeployHook` with `url` once; `400` the branch isn't in the repository, `409` without a repository, a name already used or past 5 hooks |
| DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/hooks/:hookId` 🔑 | (ADMIN) | `204`; the hook's URL stops working at once |
| POST | `/cloud/hooks/:token` | Public. Any body, ignored | `202 { build: { number, status, url } }`; `404` unknown token or deleted project, `403` suspended, `409` not connected or branch gone, `400` malformed JSON body, `413` above the body parser's limit (100 KB JSON, 4 KB text), `429` the hook's 10/min, the IP's 60/min or the month's builds used up, `502` GitHub failed, `503` GitHub App not set up |
| GET / POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/notifications` 🔑 | POST `{ name, kind, url? \| recipientIds?, buildFailed?, buildSucceeded?, previews?, formSubmitted?, formIds?, monitorAlerts?, locale? }` (ADMIN) | `{ items: CloudNotifyChannel[] }` (URLs as `hint`, never in full) / `201 CloudNotifyChannel` with `secret` once for a webhook; `400` an unsafe URL or one of the wrong service, form events on an email channel or forms not of the project, monitor alerts on an email channel, `409` past 5 |
| PATCH / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/notifications/:channelId` 🔑 | PATCH `{ name?, enabled?, url?, recipientIds?, buildFailed?, buildSucceeded?, previews?, formSubmitted?, formIds?, monitorAlerts? }` (ADMIN) | `CloudNotifyChannel` / `204`; `400` monitor alerts on an email channel (the switch also covers `audit.dropped`, decision 81) |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/notifications/:channelId/test` 🔑 | `{ event? }` (`build` default, or `form` for a Slack, Discord or webhook channel) (ADMIN) | `{ ok, error }`: a sample event sent at once |
| GET / PATCH | `/cloud/projects/:id/analytics` 🔑 | GET `?period=24h\|7d\|30d\|90d\|365d`; PATCH `{ enabled }` (ADMIN) | `{ enabled, snippet, period, totals: { visitors, pageviews }, previous: { visitors, pageviews, complete }, series: [{ t, visitors, pageviews }], pages, referrers, browsers, os, devices, events }` (each a top 10 `{ key, visitors, count }`); `previous` is `totals` for the window of the same length before the current one (4.13), `complete` false when that window starts before the 365 days kept |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/analytics.csv` 🔑 | `?period=24h\|7d\|30d\|90d\|365d` (default `7d`) | `text/csv` attachment (columns `kind,date,name,visitors,count`; up to 100,000 rows, `X-Truncated: true` when cut); 10/min |
| GET / POST / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/analytics/share` 🔑 | POST, DELETE (ADMIN) | `{ enabled, url?, token? }` (`url`, `token` only for admins; `url` is the English address) / `{ url, token }` (creates or replaces the link; same `url`) / `204` |
| GET | `/cloud/analytics/shared/:token` | Public, `?period=` (as the report), 60/min/IP, `Cache-Control: private, max-age=60` | `{ project: { name }, period, totals, series, pages, referrers, browsers, os, devices, events }`; `404` unknown token, suspended or deleted project, or analytics off |
| GET | `/cloud/analytics/script.js` | Public | The tracker (JavaScript, cached a day) |
| POST | `/cloud/analytics/event` | Public. `text/plain` JSON `{ n, u, r?, w? }` | `202` (kept or dropped, never said); 60/min/IP |
| GET | `/cloud/projects/:id/kv` 🔑 | `?prefix&cursor&limit` (1–100, default 50) | `{ items: [{ key, size, expiresAt, updatedAt }], cursor, usage: { keys, bytes, maxKeys, maxBytes } }` (`cursor` null on the last page) |
| GET / PUT / DELETE | `/cloud/projects/:id/kv/:key` 🔑 | PUT `{ value, ttl? }` | `{ key, value, size, expiresAt, updatedAt }` / the same / `204`; `404` missing, `409` past the limits, `413` a value over 64 KB |
| POST | `/cloud/projects/:id/kv/:key/incr` 🔑 | `{ by?, ttl? }` | `{ key, value }`; `409` when the value isn't an integer |
| GET | `/cloud/kv/:slug/:key` | Public; keys starting with `public:` only | The value as JSON; `404` otherwise. 120/min/IP |
| POST | `/cloud/teams/:team/billing/checkout` 🔑 | (OWNER) | `{ url }`: Stripe Checkout for PRO; `409` already PRO, `503` paid plans not set up |
